\title[POLOCENTRO comparisons]{Treatment and Control Comparisons}
\subtitle{AMC outcomes; municipal GDP; MapBiomas land cover; FAO/GAEZ suitability; propensity-score diagnostics}
\author{José Vidal Zobaran}
\institute{FGV-EPGE}
\date{\today}
\begin{document}
\begin{frame}
  \titlepage
\end{frame}
\section{Methods and scope}
\begin{frame}{The comparisons preserve each dataset's analytical grain}
\small
Every chart is descriptive and unadjusted. Propensity scores are estimated separately with and without static FAO/GAEZ controls.
\begin{columns}[T,totalwidth=\textwidth]
\column{0.24\textwidth}
\begin{block}{AMC outcomes}
One AMC-census-year row. Resident traits, migration totals, and GDP/value added per capita where matching census population is observed.
\end{block}
\column{0.24\textwidth}
\begin{block}{Municipal GDP}
One municipality-GDP-year row. Real levels, natural logs, annualized log growth, and sector shares.
\end{block}
\column{0.24\textwidth}
\begin{block}{MapBiomas}
One AMC-year from 1985--2025. Mapped-area shares and cumulative native-cover net loss are entirely post-treatment.
\end{block}
\column{0.24\textwidth}
\begin{block}{GAEZ and PSW}
Eight static high-input rainfed suitability controls augment the same 1970 census and GDP selection model.
\end{block}
\end{columns}
\end{frame}
\section{Any operational-area overlap}
\begin{frame}[plain]
\vfill
{\usebeamerfont{title}\usebeamercolor[fg]{structure}\Huge\bfseries Any operational-area overlap\par}
\vspace{1em}
{\large Treated when any non-trivial part of the unit overlaps an operational POLOCENTRO area.\par}
\vspace{2em}
\begin{columns}[T,totalwidth=\textwidth]
\column{0.3\textwidth}\centering\Huge\bfseries 110\\\normalsize treated AMCs
\column{0.3\textwidth}\centering\Huge\bfseries 125\\\normalsize treated municipalities
\column{0.3\textwidth}\centering\Huge\bfseries 69\\\normalsize time-varying outcomes
\end{columns}
\vfill
\end{frame}

\section{Operational core}

{\usebeamerfont{title}\usebeamercolor[fg]{structure}\Huge\bfseries Operational core\par}

\end{frame}

\subsection{Propensity-score weighting}
\begin{frame}{GAEZ raises max |SMD| after weighting: 0.103 to 0.144}

\section{Synthesis}
\begin{frame}{\hspace{1.5em}GAEZ changes overlap across assignments}
\small Across definitions, agronomic controls alter retained treated counts and residual weighted imbalance. Preferred outcome models should report both specifications.
